| **4.10 / 14** Land register: encumbrances, disputes, disposals (H6–H8) | ✅ | `LandEncumbrance` (servitude / caveat / bond / lease / restrictive condition / wayleave, registered and lifted), `LandDispute` + `LandDisputeStep` (ownership / boundary / encroachment / compensation / occupation; Reported → Investigation → Negotiation → Mediation → Litigation → Judgment → Resolved, forward only and dated; LD sequence) and `LandDisposal` (sale / exchange / donation / expropriation of undivided land under a council resolution; LDS sequence) - `ILandRegisterService`, pure `LandRegisterRules` (cost pro rata to area with no residue on the last piece, forward-only stages, dispute age; unit-tested), migration `AddLandRegister`, `LandParcel.ReleaseArea` + status `Disposed`. A disposal posts the cost out of the land bank, the proceeds (or the exchange parcel at fair value, created in the land bank) and the gain or loss in one unit of work; a caveat in force or an open ownership dispute refuses it. `StandClaims.AnyBlockedAsync` now also blocks stands on a caveated parcel and stands under an open land dispute (sale, lease, title, transfer, joint ownership, sectional title, allocation). API: `/api/housing/land/encumbrances(/{id}/lift)`, `/disputes(/{number}/advance)`, `/disposals`. Reports **H6** Land Disposals & Exchanges Register, **H7** Encumbrances, Servitudes & Caveats, **H8** Disputed & Encroached Land (stage as at the date from the dated steps, with age). The H series now has 80 reports. Test: `LandRegisterTests`. |
| **4.10 / 14** Scheme progress: survey, servicing, valuations (H9, H12, H13) | ✅ | `StandSurveyStep` (Pegged → DiagramLodged with the Surveyor-General → DiagramApproved, recorded for a whole layout at once, all or none, forward only; one step per stage per stand in the database), `SchemeServicingProgress` (engineer's certificates per bulk service - water, sewer, roads, electricity, storm water - % complete and stands reached, never backwards) and `LandValuation` - `ISchemeProgressService`, pure `SchemeProgressRules` (IAS 2 NRV = price − cost to complete − cost to sell, write-down only of the shortfall; a stand is serviced only once every service reaches it; unit-tested), migration `AddSchemeProgress`, API `/api/housing/survey`, `/servicing-progress`, `/land/{parcel}/valuations`. Reports **H9** Land Bank Valuation vs Carrying Value (undivided parcels at the latest valuation, unclaimed stands at the approved price rule else recorded price, flagged when there is no evidence; costs-to-sell % parameter), **H12** Survey Status (stage as at the date, longest wait at the Surveyor-General) and **H13** Servicing Status by Scheme. The H series now has 83 reports. Test: `SchemeProgressTests`. |
| **4.10 / 14** Allocation fairness & control (H34, H37, H91, H93) | ✅ | Allocation runs now record the listed applicants in the category they left out, and why (`AllocationExclusion`: suspended, or holding a live allocation from another run) - the appeal-defence record. **FIX** proposals now pick only stands free on every route (`StandClaims.UnavailableAsync`: blocks, caveated parcels, open land disputes, live allocations, cooperatives, regularised households, developer stands) instead of only unblocked ones, so an approval no longer trips over a stand that was never free; live-allocation applicants are now filtered per company. Allocation quotas: `BeneficiaryGroups` flags (women, youth, disabled, elderly, civil servants, war veterans) declared on the applicant, with youth / elderly / disabled also derived (pure `QuotaGroups`, unit-tested); `AllocationQuota` council-wide or per scheme (scheme overrides), `IAllocationQuotaService`; migration `AddAllocationExclusionsAndQuotas`; API `/api/housing/quotas`, `/applicants/{wl}/groups`. Reports **H34** Skipped Applicant / Substitution (exclusions plus declined / lapsed / cancelled offers with reasons), **H37** Quota Compliance (actual vs target per group per scheme, Met / Short), **H91** Approval Audit (runs, transfers, seniority changes, price rules, block lifts - requester, approver, turnaround, value, self-approval exceptions) and **H93** Manual Override & Exception (seniority dates changed, stand-specific prices, offers made without an approved price, blocks lifted). The H series now has 87 reports. Test: `AllocationControlReportsTests`. |

Reports **H51** Deposits Held Register (purchase and tenancy deposits held as at the date) and **H53** Cancellations & Refund Computation. The H series now has 96 reports. Test: `HousingDepositsTests`. |

| **14** Batch, serial and expiry traceability | ✅ | `Item.Tracking` (None / Batch / Serial) and `ShelfLifeDays`; `StockLotMovement` (`inv.lot_movement`) records the lots behind any costing movement, signed like it; `StockReasonCode` (`inv.reason_code`) and `StockMovement.ReasonCode` (migration `AddLotTracking`). `ILotTrackingService.RecordAsync` checks the lots account for the movement, serials are one unit each and never received while in stock, nothing leaves a lot it does not hold, and received batches default their expiry from shelf life; `SuggestPicksAsync` picks first-expiry-first-out, skipping expired lots (`LotRules`). `IInventoryService.AdjustAsync` takes an optional active reason code. Reports **INV82** adjustments by reason, **INV88** batch stock, **INV89** serial register and **INV90** expiry report (window in days). Tests: `LotRulesTests`, `LotTrackingTests`. |
| **14** In-transit transfers and consignment | ✅ | `Warehouse.IsTransit` and `ConsignmentCustomerId`; `StockTransfer` (`inv.stock_transfer`, migration `AddStockTransfers`). `IStockTransferService.DispatchAsync` moves stock from the source into a transit location under a TRF-numbered note; `ReceiveAsync` moves what arrived on to the destination and writes any shortfall off the transit location to a loss account (reason TRANSIT when defined), never more than dispatched and only once. Reports **INV91** Goods in Transit (dispatched and not received at the date, days on the road) and **INV101** Consignment Stock (our stock at customers' sites). Test: `StockTransferTests`. |
| **14** Bills of materials and assembly | ✅ | `BillOfMaterials` / `BomLine` (output per batch, component quantities with scrap %) and `AssemblyRun` / `AssemblyRunLine` (migration `AddBillsOfMaterials`). `IAssemblyService.AssembleAsync` issues each component (the standard for the planned output, or the usage keyed) to a clearing account and receives the actual output at the components' cost from the same account, so the clearing nets to zero; stray usage is refused and a run short of stock rolls back whole. `BomRules` gives requirements, rolled-up unit cost and yield. Reports **INV98** Bill of Materials Costing and **INV99** Production Yield & Usage Variance. Tests: `BomRulesTests`, `AssemblyTests`. |
| **14** Weighbridge operations | ✅ | `WeighbridgeTicket` gains direction (inbound gross-first / outbound tare-first), product item, customer / supplier, driver, site, stockpile, time in / out, manual-weight control, distance, haulage zone and charge, and the concrete batch it came from; `WeighbridgeVehicle` register (haulier, stored tare, legal maximum) and `HaulageZone` bands (migration `AddWeighbridgeOperations`). `IWeighbridgeService.FirstWeighAsync` opens a WB-numbered ticket (stored tare allowed outbound; barred vehicles refused); `SecondWeighAsync` completes it, refuses a load with no net weight, charges haulage by zone with its minimum, flags overloads and issues the net tonnes from the stockpile - atomically. Manual weights need a reason and a supervisor other than the operator (`WeighbridgeRules`). Reports **WB198**–**WB205**: tickets, daily register and tonnage, vehicle turnaround, deliveries by customer / site with haulage, manual-weight overrides, stockpile reconciliation, concrete batches (m³ produced vs delivered, variance to recipe) and haulage by zone. Tests: `WeighbridgeRulesTests`, `WeighbridgeTests`. |

Reports **FA210** Asset Verification / Count Variance (NBV at risk), **FA213** Insurance & Warranty Expiry (lapsing or lapsed cover, uninsured, under-insured, warranties) and **FA214** Maintenance Schedule & History (overdue / due soon). Test: `AssetControlTests`. |

Report **FA211** Capital Work in Progress (budget, b/f, added, capitalised, c/f, % spent). Test: `CapitalProjectTests`. |

Reports **JC192** Subcontractor Ledger, Certificates & Retention and **JC194** Plant Utilisation & Hire Recovery (weekday hours available). Test: `SubcontractTests`. |

Report **B27** Budget Block & Override. Test: `FundControlOverrideTests`. |

Tests: `LotTrackingTests`, `ContractControlTests`. |
| **14** Business document layouts | ✅ | `BusinessDocument` + `BusinessDocumentFormatter` (title, parties, reference fields, lines with or without prices, totals, notes, signature lines - fixed-width text) and `ShelfLabelFormatter` (boxed labels with price and a Code 39 style barcode line). `IDocumentLayoutService` renders the quotation, order confirmation, delivery note and packing note (with load weight), purchase requisition (with expense votes and budget sign-off) and goods received note from their records, and shelf labels from a price list. Tests: `BusinessDocumentFormatterTests`, `DocumentLayoutTests`. |
| **14** Debit notes, bills, fee invoices and payment certificates | ✅ | `IDocumentLayoutService` adds the supplier debit note (from the supplier return's stock movement and credit), the municipal account for a stand and month (services billed in the month's runs, balance brought forward, total due), the school fee invoice (the structure's compulsory items less bursary, or the transport / boarding tag) and the interim payment certificate (work done, materials on site, previously certified, retention, net due). Test: `DocumentLayoutTests`. |
| **UI** Operations console (desktop) | ✅ | `OperationsWindow` (menu **Operations**) gives the desktop a screen for each of this cycle's back-office actions, grouped by module: POS refunds and till events; lot/serial capture, pick suggestions, reason-coded adjustments, transfer dispatch/receipt and assembly runs; weighbridge first/second weigh; asset counts, planned maintenance and capital projects (CWIP → capitalise); variations, BOQ measurement, timesheets, materials issues, subcontracts and plant logs; attendance, overtime approval, leave, establishment, employee documents, disciplinary, training, appraisals and garnishees; cheque register and supplier statements; proof of delivery and student wallets; and a **Document Printer** for every business document layout. Each action is an `Operation` (form fields → tested application service, records looked up by code) with a live list beneath (open tickets, transfers in transit, overtime awaiting approval, cheque register…). |
| **11B** Payroll corrections after commit (PR8–PR10) | ✅ | `IPayrollAdjustmentService` (on `PayrollService`): supplementary runs for a committed period (`Payroll.PeriodNotRun`, `DuplicateRun`; negative amounts swap journal sides), formally approved reversals (engine `ReverseAsync`, negative payslips, maker-checker `Payroll.SameApprover`, once only, a reversed period may be run again), backpay for a retrospective basic-salary change (recalculates each paid period, net of earlier corrections, pays the difference as a `Backpay` run, keeps `BackpayLine`s, updates the salary) and the legacy payroll import (`LegacyPayslipLine`). Payslips carry `RunKind` and `RunReference`; the run register carries kind, document, reason and reversal; one live regular run per period is a filtered unique index. Reports PR8 Parallel Run Comparison, PR9 Supplementary Runs & Reversal Register, PR10 Backpay. Desktop: four HR & Attendance operations. Migration `AddPayrollAdjustments`. Test: `PayrollAdjustmentTests`. |
| **11B** Recoveries through payroll (PR23, PR24) | ✅ | `PayElement.Recovers` (StaffPurchases / HousingRent) with formula variables `STAFFPURCHASES` (staff-account tenders at the till - `PosTenderRequest.StaffEmployeeId`, credit tender of an active employee - less staff deductions already made) and `HOUSINGRENT` (the balance of the tenancy account linked by `Employee.HousingTenancyId`, as at the pay date). Rent deducted is receipted to the tenancy's AR account through the sales ledger (Dr the deduction's clearing account) in the same unit as the run; a reversal reverses the receipt. Backpay ignores recovery elements. Reports PR23 Staff Purchase Deductions Reconciliation and PR24 Housing Rent Deduction Reconciliation. Migration `AddPayrollRecoveries`. Test: `PayrollRecoveryTests`. |
| **11B** Multi-currency tax split (PR35) | ✅ | `PayElement.PaidInCurrency` marks earnings paid in a second currency (amounts stay in base) and `TaxTable.Currency` adds currency-specific tables (base-table reports ignore them). PR35 Multi-Currency Tax Split: per employee and period with foreign-currency earnings, each currency's earnings converted at the pay date's daily rate, its share, the bands applied and the tax attributable - on the currency's own table when one is in force, otherwise the base table's tax on the whole remuneration apportioned by share - with the tax deducted apportioned. Migration `AddPayrollCurrencySplit`. Test: `PayrollCurrencySplitTests`. The §14 O payroll series is complete. |
| **12** Variance decomposition (B35) | ✅ | Budget lines carry an optional budgeted `Quantity` and, for foreign-currency lines, `Currency` + `PlannedRate` (`NewBudgetLine` validates them: `Budget.LineQuantity`, `Budget.PlannedRate`); `ActualVolume` records actual volumes (`IBudgetingService.RecordActualVolumeAsync`). B35 Variance Decomposition splits each budget key's year-to-date variance into volume ((actual − budget qty) × budget price), FX (foreign amounts posted × (actual − planned rate)) and price / rate (the rest), always adding to the total. Migration `AddBudgetVolumes`. Test: `VarianceDecompositionTests`. The §14 backlog is complete. |
| **11B** Net-pay protection (§4.15.4) | ✅ | `Company.MinimumTakeHomeAmount` / `MinimumTakeHomePercent` set a floor (the higher of the two); `PayElement.SuspensionPriority` marks deductions that may be suspended (0 = never - tax, pension, court orders; highest first). The run (and the preview and payslip) cuts suspendable deductions until net pay reaches the floor, carries the shortfall forward as arrears (`DeductionArrear`), attempts it again with the next run and records each suspension and recovery; a run reversal undoes its arrears; recoveries (staff purchases, rent) keep their own balances. Report PR71 Net-Pay Protection & Deduction Arrears. Desktop: HR & Attendance → Set net-pay protection. Migration `AddNetPayProtection`. Test: `NetPayProtectionTests`. |
| **11B** Pay groups - multiple payrolls per company (§4.15.6) | ✅ | `Employee.PayGroup` (MONTHLY / WEEKLY / CASUAL…, null = the default payroll); `IPayrollService.RunGroupAsync` pays only the group's active employees (`RunAsync` runs the default payroll); payslips and the run register carry the group, the period guard and its filtered unique index are per group, and a reversal frees only that group's period. PR2 totals per pay group. Desktop: HR & Attendance → Run payroll / Set an employee's pay group. Migration `AddPayGroups`. Test: `PayGroupTests`. |
| **11B** Maker-checker payroll (§4.15.6) | ✅ | `Company.PayrollApprovalRequired` (a direct run is then refused, `Payroll.ApprovalRequired`) and `PayrollSecondApprovalVariancePercent`. `IPayrollApprovalService`: `CalculateRunAsync` (re-runnable; supersedes the earlier draft; records totals, the movement from the group's last run and an exception report - negative / zero net, large per-employee movement, deductions suspended by net-pay protection), `ApproveRunAsync` (not whoever calculated; a second, different approver above the threshold) and `CommitRunAsync` (recalculates and refuses `Payroll.ChangedSinceApproval`). `PayrollDraft` keeps who calculated, approved and committed; PR7 now shows them. The run itself is split into prepare / commit. Desktop: Calculate / Approve / Commit payroll. Migration `AddPayrollApproval`. Test: `PayrollApprovalTests`. |
| **11B** Payroll variable input (§4.15.6) | ✅ | `PayrollVariable` per employee, element and period: units for a rate-per-unit element or an amount replacing the calculated one; an element the employee does not normally have is paid for that period only. `IPayrollVariableService`: `SetAsync` (keyed), `PreviewImportAsync` (every CSV line with its error - unknown employee or element, inactive, units on a non-unit element, both / neither given, duplicate, period already paid) and `ImportAsync` (refused whole on any error; replaces the period's earlier import; a keyed variable wins). The run, payslip and backpay use the period's variables. Report PR72 Variable Input Register. Desktop: Key a pay variable / Import pay variables. Migration `AddPayrollVariables`. Test: `PayrollVariableTests`. |
| **11B** Third-party payments (§4.15.5) | ✅ | `StatutoryScheme.SupplierId` and `GarnisheeOrder.SupplierId` link payees to AP suppliers (new `SchemeKind.TaxAuthority`). `IPayrollRemittanceService.RaiseRemittancesAsync` raises a run's supplier invoices - one per scheme and liability account (employee and employer parts together), one per garnishee order - Dr the payroll liability, Cr AP, so remittances are paid through payables; once per run (`Payroll.RemittancesRaised`), never for a reversed run. `Company.RaisePayrollRemittances` raises them with every committed regular run. `RemittanceInvoice` records each; report PR73 Third-Party Invoices Raised (with what is still outstanding). Desktop: Raise third-party invoices. Migration `AddPayrollRemittances`. Test: `PayrollRemittanceTests`. |
| **4.15.7** Bank EFT file layouts | ✅ | Pure `BankFileFormatter` (Application/Banking): header / detail / trailer templates with `{Field[,width][:format]}` placeholders - left / right / zero padding with truncation for fixed-width files, `cents`, .NET formats - plus `HashTotal` (last 11 digits of each account, mod 10^12) and `UnknownFields` validation. `BankFileLayout` (cb.bank_file_layout) stores a bank's templates. `IBankFileService`: `SaveLayoutAsync` (refuses unknown fields) and `PayrollFilesAsync` (a run's net pay, one file per beneficiary bank and currency, bank-transfer splits to their own accounts, held salaries and non-bank employees left out, `Bank.MissingAccount`). `PaymentRunRequest.EftLayoutCode` writes the supplier payment run's EFT file in a layout (checked before anything is paid). Desktop: Banking → Save a bank file layout / Write payroll bank files. Migration `AddBankFileLayouts`. Tests: `BankFileFormatterTests` (unit), `BankFileTests`. |
| **4.15.7** Employee self-service | ✅ | `IEmployeeSelfService` finds the employee through the signed-in user (`Employee.UserId`) - never an id from the caller: profile (account masked), payslip history by period, the committed payslip as text (all runs of the period, logged as a print), the year's tax certificate figures (shared `TaxLines.IsTax`), leave balances with pending days, leave applications (refused beyond balance less pending) and payment-detail change requests (`LeaveApplication`, `DetailChangeRequest`). HR decides with maker-checker (`Ess.OwnRequest`): approval takes the leave through the attendance service or applies the change. API: `/api/ess/me`, `/payslips`, `/payslips/{period}`, `/tax-certificate`, `/leave` (GET/POST), `/detail-change`, and `/api/hr/self-service/pending` + decide endpoints, all using the token's subject. Desktop: Decide self-service requests. Migration `AddEmployeeSelfService`. Test: `EmployeeSelfServiceTests`. |
| **4.15.7** Password-protected payslip PDFs | ✅ | Pure `PdfTextDocument` (Application/Documents) writes fixed-width text as a Courier PDF, optionally protected with the PDF Standard Security Handler R4 (AES-128 / AESV2 streams; key and O/U values per ISO 32000-1 algorithms 1, 2, 3, 5) - verified with an independent decryption in the unit tests and with xpdf `pdftotext` (refuses no / wrong password, opens with the right one). `Company.PayslipPasswordRule` (national ID, date of birth, employee number, none) and `Employee.Email`. `IPayslipDistributionService`: `PayslipPdfAsync` and `EmailRunAsync` (each employee of a run queued their PDF through the messaging service; no-address and no-password employees listed). API: `/api/ess/payslips/{period}/pdf`, `/api/payroll/runs/{run}/email-payslips`. Migration `AddPayslipDistribution`. Tests: `PdfTextDocumentTests` (unit), `PayslipDistributionTests`. |
| **4.15.9 / 4.15.10** Payroll cost analysis and own-pay block | ✅ | The payroll journal debits earnings and employer costs by each employee's department and cost centre, matched to the GL `Department` / `CostCentre` masters by code or name (unmatched stays unanalysed); liabilities and net pay stay lump. Nobody can calculate, approve, commit or approve the reversal of a run that includes their own employee record (`Payroll.OwnPay`, naming the conflict). Test: `PayrollAnalysisTests`. |
| **4.15.3** PAYE from tax tables (cumulative, bonus spreading) | ✅ | New `CalculationBasis.TaxTable`: the effective-dated table in force on the pay date (`PayElement.TaxTableCode`, or the base table). Per period: the period's regular taxable pay on the bands, with earnings flagged `IsAnnualPayment` (bonus, 13th cheque) taxed annualised and apportioned. `CumulativeTax`: year-to-date taxable on the bands times the period number less the tax already deducted this year (refunds follow). Per-employee formula variables `YTDTAXABLE`, `TAXPERIOD`, `YTD_<code>` and `ANNUALTAXABLE`; backpay recalculates tax the same way; `Payroll.NoTaxTable` when none is in force. Migration `AddTaxTableBasis`. Test: `TaxTableBasisTests`. |
| **4.15.9** Salary accrual and labour capitalisation | ✅ | `IPayrollAccountingService.AccrueSalariesAsync` accrues basic salary for everyone employed but not paid in the month (the PR63 list) through the accrual service, auto-reversed on the 1st of the next month; `CapitaliseTimesheetsAsync` moves a contract's timesheet labour up to a date into its WIP (Dr WIP, Cr the payroll cost recovered), each timesheet once (`JobTimesheet.CapitalisedJournal`). Desktop: Accrue unpaid salaries / Capitalise timesheet labour to WIP. Migration `AddTimesheetCapitalisation`. Test: `PayrollAccountingTests`. |

The monthly run charges (cost − residual) × the month's units ÷ total units, nothing when idle, never below residual. Desktop: Fixed Assets → Record asset usage. Migration `AddUnitsOfProduction`. Test: `UnitsOfProductionTests`. |

Desktop: Create a customer contract / Call off against a contract. Migration `AddCustomerContracts`. Test: `CustomerContractTests`. |

Desktop: Drop-ship order lines. Migration `AddDropShipments`. Test: `DropShipTests`. |

POS lines carry `PromotionDiscount` / `DiscountAccountCode` / `PromotionCode`: the sale posts revenue at gross and the discount to its account, voids and refunds reverse both shares. Desktop: Till pickup / paid-out / float top-up. Migration `AddTillCashAndRounding`. Test: `TillCashTests`. |

Pure `WbsRollup`: actual from captured costs by head, committed = open subcontract value (`Subcontract.CostHead`, set on let, certificates captured under it), ETC = site estimate or budget left (≥ 0), forecast final cost and variance, phases summed, unmapped heads on an unassigned line, numeric code order. Certificate payable = gross − retention − recovery − withholding (`Job.DeductionsExceedGross`); deductions journal Dr job cost / Cr advance / Cr WHT payable. Certificates record `AdvanceRecovered`/`Withheld`. Desktop: Contracts → pay a subcontract advance / set subcontract withholding tax. Test: `SubcontractAdvanceTests`. |
| **4.2** Disassembly and blind recounts | ✅ | `IAssemblyService.DisassembleAsync`: finished goods issued to the clearing account at cost, components recovered (BOM quantity without scrap, or as given) received from it, the cost split by recovered qty × average cost (standard cost fallback; pure `BomRules.SplitCost`, last part takes the rounding) so clearing nets to zero; run marked `IsDisassembly`, numbered DSM; `Inv.NothingRecovered`. Stocktakes: `RecountTolerancePercent` set at start; counts record the counter; a count whose variance exceeds the tolerance must be recounted by someone else (`Stocktake.SameCounter` / `NoRecountDue` / `AlreadyRecounted`), the recount replaces the count (first kept), posting refused while recounts are due (`Stocktake.RecountsDue`) and now one atomic unit. `CountSheetAsync` = blind sheet without system quantities. Desktop: disassemble, start stocktake, count, recount, post, blind count-sheet list. Tests: `BomSplitCostTests`, `DisassemblyAndRecountTests`. |

Pure `DeliverySchedule.Match`: receipts fill the earliest deliveries first; unscheduled lines fall due on required-by (or order date); amendments extend the last delivery or trim the latest. `ExpediteAsync(asOf, daysAhead)`: outstanding deliveries on open orders due by the horizon, most late first. Desktop: Purchasing → schedule a PO line's deliveries, expediting list. Tests: unit + `DeliveryScheduleTests`. |

The API access check now applies scopes to `companyId`, `branchId`, `warehouseId`, `terminalId`, `contractId`/`jobId` (by id) and `costCentre`, `department`, `ward` (by code), in the query or the route. | **4.18.5** Budget gate on requisitions, reservations (part 1) | ✅ | `BudgetEnforcementRule` (`bud.enforcement_rule`: department or any, account prefix or any, from a value; `BudgetAction` Allow / Warn / RequireApproval / Block); pure `BudgetGate` (most specific rule: department, longest prefix, highest band; a document takes the strictest action among lines that overrun). `IBudgetGateService`: set rules, check spend (available = full-year approved budget - revised if active, else original - less the year's actuals and open commitments), commit and release by document reference, reserve (earmark, refused at RequireApproval/Block). Requisitions: lines carry an expense account (`PurchaseRequisitionLineRequest.ExpenseAccountCode`); submission and approval are budget gates (`Budget.Blocked`; `Budget.ApprovalNeeded` unless the approver holds `Budget.OverrideBlock`), the outcome is kept on the requisition, approval commits the spend and conversion moves the commitment to the order. Desktop: Purchasing → budget enforcement rule / check or reserve budget. Tests: unit + `BudgetGateTests`. |
| **4.18.5/4.18.7** Budget gate on orders and invoices, commitment reconciliation (part 2) | ✅ | PO lines carry an expense account (`PurchaseOrderLineRequest.ExpenseAccountCode`, `PurchaseOrderLine.ExpenseAccountCode`); raising an order is a budget gate (`Budget.Blocked`; `Budget.ApprovalNeeded` unless `BudgetApprovedByUserId` may override) and commits budgeted lines under the order number, atomically. Invoice matching re-checks only spend above the order price (`MatchInvoiceRequest.BudgetApprovedByUserId`) and uses up the commitment line by line (`BudgetCommitment.Reduce`, `IBudgetGateService.ReduceAsync`); cancelling an order releases it. Requisition conversion is one unit: releases the requisition commitment, then the order commits its own (approver carried over). `ReconcileAsync` (nightly report): committed-but-nothing-on-order, on-order-with-no-commitment, amount differences, commitments of requisitions no longer approved. Desktop: Purchasing → reconcile budget commitments (release a stale reference). Caveat: stock lines receipt into inventory, so budget actuals arrive when the stock is used. Test: `BudgetGateOrderTests`. |

Pure `BudgetSpread` (even, seasonal from the prior year's actuals, custom weights, S-curve, working days; to the cent, last period takes the rounding) via `SpreadAsync` on budgets in preparation. `BuildStaffBudgetAsync`: approved establishment posts × cost per post, + increment, − vacancy factor, spread evenly, employer contributions to their own account; re-run re-computes. Re-phasing (`BudgetRephase`, `bud.rephase`): request on an approved budget (≤ what the period holds), approve/reject by someone else, applied as a −/+ pair of `RephaseId` lines so the annual total and the seal are unchanged. Desktop: Budgeting → set an assumption (preview/apply), driver-based line, spread, staff budget, re-phase. Tests: `BudgetSpreadTests`, `BudgetDriverSpreadTests`. |

| **5.2** Sign-in hardening | ✅ | Pure `Totp` (RFC 6238 HMAC-SHA1, 6 digits, ±1 step; Base32 secrets; otpauth URI; RFC vectors tested). `IAuthenticationService`: `BeginTwoFactorAsync` / `ConfirmTwoFactorAsync` / `DisableTwoFactorAsync` (`User.TwoFactorSecret`); `AuthenticateWithCodeAsync` - password sign-in for a two-factor user needs the code (`AuthenticationOutcome.TwoFactorRequired`; a wrong code counts as a failed attempt; till PIN sign-in exempt). `AuthenticationOptions` (server binds the `Authentication` section, all off by default): `PasswordHistoryDepth` (`PasswordHistory`, `sec.password_history`; change refuses the current and recent passwords; admin resets remember the replaced one), `PasswordMaxAge` (`User.PasswordChangedUtc`; an expired password signs in with MustChangePassword), `SessionIdleTimeout` (`LoginEvent.LastSeenUtc`; `TouchSessionAsync` closes an idle session so the client locks), `MaxConcurrentSessions` with refuse-new or end-oldest (`TooManySessions`). API: login accepts `oneTimeCode` and reports `twoFactorRequired`; `POST /api/me/two-factor` and `/confirm`. Desktop login has an authenticator-code box. Tests: `TotpTests`, `AuthenticationHardeningTests`. |
| **5.1** Field-level sensitivity | ✅ | Pure `FieldSensitivity`: classifies field/column names as Cost, Margin (margin, gross profit, markup), Salary (salary, gross/net/basic pay) or CreditLimit - cost centre, cost head and account-code names excluded; `HiddenFor` (cost unless some grant carries ViewCost, margin unless ViewMargin, salary unless any Payroll permission, credit limit unless Sales.CreditControl; administrators see all); strips JSON at any depth and report rows. Server middleware strips hidden fields from JSON API responses for signed-in users; `IAuditedReportRunner` leaves hidden columns out of report output (`IReportRunner.RenderAsync` overload with hidden fields). Caveat: only recognisable names are caught (e.g. a column called Extended is not). Tests: unit + `FieldSensitivityTests` (BOM costing without/with cost rights). |

Each decision keeps a JSON `WorkflowApproval.Snapshot` (type, reference, value, stage, attributes). `ActManyAsync` bulk-acts item by item (`BulkActionResult`). `SetStageRulesAsync` (`Workflow.ConditionInvalid`). Desktop: Approvals → approve several at once, set a stage's rules, set a user's manager. Tests: `WorkflowConditionTests`, `WorkflowRulesTests`. |
| **6** Audit events and the database second net | ✅ | `IAuditService.RecordAsync(AuditEvent)` writes non-data events into the hash-chained change log (overrides need a reason - `Audit.ReasonRequired`). Recorded now: report prints/reprints and exports (`AuditedReportRunner`), role assignment/removal and group membership (PermissionChange), activation and password resets (ConfigChange), licence installs (LicenceChange), sign-in and sign-out (Login/Logout with session and terminal). Second net: migration `DatabaseChangeSecondNet` adds append-only `audit.db_change` and `audit.capture_db_change()` AFTER triggers on sec.user, sec.role, sec.role_permission, sec.user_role, inv.price_list_line, lic.installed_licence, mun.tariff, mun.meter (old/new rows as jsonb minus password/PIN hashes, authenticator secret and security stamp; session user, application name, client address). The application's connections are named One4All, so `DatabaseChangesAsync(outsideApplicationOnly)` isolates direct SQL. Desktop: System Manager → review direct database changes. Test: `AuditEventTests` (events logged, chain intact, a psql edit caught and flagged, secrets absent). |
| **10** Webhook publisher | ✅ | `WebhookSubscription` / `WebhookDelivery` (`sys.webhook_subscription`, `sys.webhook_delivery` outbox). `IWebhookService`: subscribe (known event or *, http(s) URL, secret ≥ 16 chars), unsubscribe, `PublishAsync` (queues a delivery per active subscriber inside the caller's unit of work - never lost, never sent for a rolled-back change), `DispatchDueAsync` (POST JSON with X-One4All-Event / -Delivery / -Signature: sha256=HMAC of the body; 2xx delivered, else retried at 1 min, 5 min, 30 min, 2 h, 12 h, given up after 6 attempts), retry a failed delivery. Events: sale.completed (POS), invoice.posted and payment.received (sales ledger), bill.generated (municipal billing run). Pure `WebhookRules` (signature, back-off). Server `WebhookHost` dispatches every minute. Desktop: System Manager → manage webhooks (deliveries list). Tests: `WebhookRulesTests`, `WebhookTests`. |
| **9** Backup and restore | ✅ | `IBackupService` (`BackupOptions`: folder, off-site folder, pg tools folder - auto-found on the PATH or the newest PostgreSQL install - retention; server binds the `Backup` section): `BackupAsync` runs pg_dump (custom format; password passed through PGPASSWORD in the child's environment only), records `BackupRecord` (`sys.backup`: file, size, SHA-256, reason, status) and copies it off-site; `VerifyAsync` checks the file exists, matches its hash and reads back with `pg_restore --list` (Verified / Corrupt); `PruneAsync` deletes good backups beyond the newest N; `RestoreToNewDatabaseAsync` restores a verified backup only into a new, validly named database that is not the live one, with the confirmation `RESTORE name`. Scheduled tasks: `system.backup` (take, verify, prune) and `budget.reconcile-commitments` (the nightly commitment reconciliation). Desktop: System Manager → back up or restore the database. Test: `BackupTests` (real pg_dump/pg_restore: backup, off-site copy, verify, restore into a throwaway database, tamper detection, prune). |

Desktop: `IdleLock` locks after `ONE4ALL_LOCK_MINUTES` without input (default 10, 0 off), touches the server session every two minutes of activity and locks at once when the server has closed it; File → Lock (Ctrl+L); `LockWindow` hides every window until the password (and code) unlocks it, or signs out. File → Sign-in Security: `TwoFactorWindow` starts enrolment (key in groups of four and the otpauth link, both copyable), confirms with a code, and turns two-factor off only after re-entering the password and a current code.
