Read-side via `RegistersReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a customer with a 5,000 limit / 45-day terms / on-hold and a 60-day supplier render in their respective registers. |
| **4++** Customer credit-exposure report | ✅ | New **AR-CREDIT** report (`ITradingReports.CreditExposureAsync` + `CreditExposureReport`): each customer's credit limit, current receivable balance, remaining credit, and on-hold / over-limit flags (0 limit = unlimited) as at a date - the review counterpart to the credit control enforced on invoice posting. Read-side via `TradingReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a customer with a 1,000 limit and a 600 balance shows 400 available / not over-limit; a 500-limit on-hold customer with no balance still appears flagged. |
| **4++** Settlement discount on receipt | ✅ | New `ISalesLedgerService.SettleWithDiscountAsync` settles a customer invoice when the customer pays early: posts the cash receipt (Dr bank / Cr AR) and the discount (Dr discounts-allowed / Cr AR), then allocates both against the invoice so it fully clears. Refuses non-positive amounts or a cash+discount total exceeding the outstanding. No migration. Test: a 1,000 invoice settled with 980 cash + 20 discount clears the invoice (1100 Dr 980, 4700 Dr 20), the customer drops off aged debtors, over-settlement refused; TB balances. |
| **4++** Supplier settlement discount | ✅ | New `IPurchaseLedgerService.PayWithDiscountAsync` - the AP mirror of the AR settlement discount: pays a supplier invoice early by posting the cash payment (Dr AP / Cr bank) and the discount received (Dr AP / Cr discounts-received income), allocating both to clear the invoice. Refuses non-positive amounts or a cash+discount total exceeding the outstanding. No migration; Discounts Received (4950) added to the Scenario. Test: a 500 invoice paid with 485 cash + 15 discount clears (1100 Cr 485, 4950 Cr 15), supplier drops off aged creditors, over-settlement refused; TB balances. |
| **4++** Creditor write-back | ✅ | New `IPurchaseLedgerService.WriteBackAsync` - the AP counterpart to the AR bad-debt write-off: releases an outstanding creditor invoice the business will not pay by posting Dr AP control / Cr a write-back income account (as a "payment" whose money side is income) and allocating it against the invoice so it settles and stops ageing. Refuses a missing reason/account or an item that is not an outstanding creditor balance. No migration. Test: a 150 stale supplier invoice is written back to sundry income (4400 Cr 150), the invoice settles, the supplier drops off aged creditors, a repeat write-back is refused; TB balances. |
| **4++** Bank charges & interest | ✅ | New `IBankingService.PostBankChargeAsync` (Dr bank-charges expense / Cr bank) and `PostInterestReceivedAsync` (Dr bank / Cr interest income) post routine cash-book entries through the engine; both refuse a non-positive amount. No migration; Bank Charges (6900) + Interest Income (4800) accounts added to the Scenario. Test: a 35 charge and 12 interest post (6900 Dr 35, 4800 Cr 12, bank net Cr 23); non-positive refused; TB balances. |
| **4++** Payments-due schedule report | ✅ | New **AP-DUE** report (`ITradingReports.PaymentsDueAsync` + `PaymentsDueReport`): every outstanding supplier invoice ordered by due date, with days-until-due (negative when overdue) and the total - the line-level companion to CB-FLOW, driving the payment run. Read-side via `TradingReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a 400 overdue + 250 future invoice list earliest-due first with the right signs and total 650. |
| **4++** Cash-flow forecast report | ✅ | New **CB-FLOW** report (`ITradingReports.CashFlowForecastAsync` + `CashFlowForecastReport`): expected receipts (open receivables) and payments (open payables) bucketed by due date relative to an as-at date - Overdue / Due 0-30 / 31-60 / 61-90 / 90+ - with net and running net per bucket, a short-term treasury projection. Read-side via `TradingReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a 500 overdue + 1,000 due-0-30 receivable and an 800 due-31-60 payable bucket correctly with running net 500 → 1,500 → 700. |
| **5++** Requisition register report | ✅ | New **POP-REQ** report lists purchase requisitions (number, supplier, date, estimated value, status), newest first. Read-side via `RegistersReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a 5×30 draft requisition shows 150.00 / Draft with the supplier name. |
| **10++** Retention release | ✅ | New `IJobCostingService.ReleaseRetentionAsync` fills the construction gap after the WIP report: at the end of the defects-liability period it moves held retention from the retention-receivable holding account onto the client's AR so it ages and becomes collectible (an AR invoice whose revenue side is the retention-receivable account → Dr AR control / Cr retention receivable), and reduces the retention held. Refuses a non-positive amount or more than the retention held. No migration. Test: a 25,000 retention held is released - retention-held → 0, holding account (1250) nets to zero, the debtor rises from 225,000 to 250,000; zero and over-held releases refused; TB balances. |
| **10++** Job WIP report | ✅ | New **JOB-WIP** report: per construction contract, revenue recognised on cost-to-cost completion (contract value × % complete) versus certified-to-date, giving the WIP position - a contract asset when under-billed (positive) or a contract liability when over-billed (negative), the IFRS-15 angle distinct from JOB-PROF. Read-side via `PosJobReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a 1,000,000 contract 25% complete recognises 250,000, certified 150,000 → WIP 100,000 (under-billed). |
| **5++** Goods return to supplier | ✅ | New `IProcurementService.ReturnToSupplierAsync` (clears the long-deferred item): removes returned goods from stock at the current moving-average cost (Cr stock control), raises an allocatable AP debit note at the agreed return price (Dr AP control), and books the difference between the return price and the average cost to a **purchase-price-variance** account (Cr on a favourable, Dr on an unfavourable variance). The debit note reduces the supplier balance and allocates against the invoice. New `StockMovementType.SupplierReturn`; Purchase Price Variance (5400) added to the Scenario; no migration. Test: receive 100@10, invoice 1,000, return 20@12 → stock −200, AP debit-note 240, PPV Cr 40, stock on hand 80/800, supplier nets to 760; zero-qty and over-on-hand returns refused; TB balances. |
| **5++** Purchase requisitions | ✅ | New `pop` entities `PurchaseRequisition`/`PurchaseRequisitionLine` (Draft → Submitted → Approved/Rejected → Converted; ledger-neutral) + `IPurchaseRequisitionService`: `CreateAsync` (refuses no-lines / non-positive qty / unknown supplier), `SubmitAsync`, `ApproveAsync`, `RejectAsync`, and `ConvertToPurchaseOrderAsync` (approved-only → creates a PO via `IProcurementService.CreatePurchaseOrderAsync` with the requisition's lines, marks it Converted, records the order id) - the buy-side mirror of sales quotations. `AddPurchaseRequisitions` migration. Test: a 100×2 requisition converts after submit+approve into PO PO-REQ1; approve-before-submit, convert-before-approve, convert-after-convert and empty requisitions are refused. |
| **7++** Sales quotations | ✅ | New `sop` entities `Quotation`/`QuotationLine` (Draft → Sent → Accepted/Declined → Converted; ledger-neutral) + `IQuotationService`: `CreateAsync` (refuses no-lines / non-positive qty / valid-until before quote date / unknown customer), `SendAsync`, `AcceptAsync`, `DeclineAsync`, and `ConvertToOrderAsync` (accepted-only → creates a sales order via `ISalesOrderService.CreateOrderAsync` with the quote's lines, marks the quote Converted and records the order id). `AddSalesQuotations` migration. Test: a 5×10 quote converts after acceptance into order SO-Q1 with the matching line; convert-before-accept, convert-after-convert, empty and invalid-validity quotes are all refused. |
| **4++** Recurring sales invoices | ✅ | New `ar` entity `RecurringSalesInvoice` (standing subscription/retainer/rent template: customer, amount, tax, revenue account, interval months, next-run date, active) + `IRecurringSalesInvoiceService`: `CreateAsync` (validates positive amount, non-negative tax, positive interval, known customer), `GenerateDueAsync` (bills every active template due on/before a cut-off - posting a real AR invoice via `ISalesLedgerService` numbered reference-yyyyMM and advancing the next run, catching up multiple periods), `SetActiveAsync`. `AddRecurringSalesInvoices` migration. Test: a 100/month template billed as at mid-April posts three catch-up invoices (Feb/Mar/Apr), next-run advances to May, aged debtors shows 300, a re-run bills nothing, and suspending stops further billing. |
| **4++** Recurring purchase invoices | ✅ | New `ap` entity `RecurringPurchaseInvoice` (standing rent/subscription/service-contract template) + `IRecurringPurchaseInvoiceService`: `CreateAsync` (validates positive amount, non-negative tax, positive interval, known supplier), `GenerateDueAsync` (bills every active template due on/before a cut-off - posting a real AP invoice via `IPurchaseLedgerService` numbered reference-yyyyMM and advancing the next run, catching up multiple periods), `SetActiveAsync` - the AP mirror of recurring sales invoices. `AddRecurringPurchaseInvoices` migration. Test: a 200/month rent template billed as at mid-April posts three catch-up invoices, next-run advances to May, aged creditors shows 600, re-run bills nothing, non-positive/zero-interval templates refused. |
| **4++** Recurring-invoice register report | ✅ | New **AR-RECUR** report lists recurring-invoice templates (reference, customer, net, tax, interval months, next-run date, active flag). Read-side via `RegistersReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a 250/month retainer template shows 250.00 / Yes with the client name. |
| **4++** Recurring-purchase register report | ✅ | New **AP-RECUR** report lists recurring supplier-invoice templates (reference, supplier, net, tax, interval, next-run, active) - the AP counterpart to AR-RECUR. Read-side via `RegistersReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a 90/month hosting template shows 90.00 / Yes with the supplier name. |
| **5++** Stock ageing report | ✅ | New **INV-AGE** report lists on-hand items with their value and an age band since last movement (0-30 / 31-60 / 61-90 / 90+ days) as at a date - distinct from the single-cutoff INV-SLOW. Read-side via `VerticalReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: stock received 2026-01-01, valued at cost, falls in the 90+ band as at 2026-06-30. |
| **5++** Item master report | ✅ | New **INV-ITEMS** report lists stock items (code, description, unit, reorder level, default revenue/COS accounts, active), ordered by code. Read-side via `VerticalReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a widget with UoM EA / reorder 25 renders active. |
| **5++** Inventory ABC analysis report | ✅ | New **INV-ABC** report ranks items by stock value with the running cumulative percentage and a Pareto class - A (top ~80% of value), B (next ~15%), C (tail). Read-side via `VerticalReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: items valued 800/150/50 classify A/B/C at cumulative 80/95/100%. |
| **7++** Sales back-orders report | ✅ | New **SOP-BO** report lists sales-order lines not yet fully despatched (order, customer, date, item, ordered/despatched/outstanding quantities), excluding cancelled orders. Read-side via `RegistersReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a 10-unit order with 4 despatched shows outstanding 6. |
| **7++** Quotation register report | ✅ | New **SOP-QUOTE** report lists sales quotations (number, customer, quote/valid-until dates, value, status), newest first. Read-side via `RegistersReportDataProvider`, seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a 4×25 draft quote shows 100.00 / Draft with customer name. |
| **11++** Fixed-asset disposals report | ✅ | New `fa` entity `AssetDisposal` (asset, date, proceeds, NBV at disposal, gain/loss, journal) written by `IFixedAssetService.DisposeAsync` when an asset is disposed, plus the **FA-DISP** report (newest first) via `VerticalReportDataProvider`. Preserves disposal history after the asset is marked disposed. `AddAssetDisposals` migration. Test: an asset with NBV 1,000 sold for 1,200 records a 200 gain and appears in FA-DISP. |
| **11++** Fixed-asset maintenance log | ✅ | New `fa` entity `AssetMaintenance` (asset, date, work done, informational cost, provider - operational history, no GL) + `IFixedAssetService.RecordMaintenanceAsync` (refuses empty description, negative cost, unknown or disposed asset) and the **FA-MAINT** register report (newest first) via `VerticalReportDataProvider`. `AddAssetMaintenance` migration. Test: two service events logged against a van appear in FA-MAINT with cost/provider; blank-description, negative-cost and unknown-asset logs are refused. |
| **11++** Depreciation forecast report | ✅ | New **FA-FCST** report (`IFixedAssetService.DepreciationForecastAsync` + `DepreciationForecastReport`): projects monthly depreciation across all active assets for N months from a start month - per-period total charge and aggregate closing NBV - by simulating each asset (straight-line or reducing-balance, capped at residual) without posting. Read-side via `VerticalReportDataProvider` (now takes `IFixedAssetService`), seeded + `ReportRunner` fallback; no migration. Test: a 12,000 asset over 12 months forecasts 1,000/month, NBV to zero by month 12 and 0 thereafter; refuses non-positive months. |

**Phase 11B status:** Formula-driven payroll engine, payslips, the payroll journal and employee loans through the central engine. Deferred depth: effective-dated tax tables & YTD cumulative PAYE, statutory returns, multi-currency taxation, leave & IAS 19 leave provision, automatic loan-repayment via a payroll deduction element & third-party remittance to AP, split-pay & bank-detail dual-auth, establishment/post control, self-payslip SoD block (needs current-user gate), net-pay protection & arrears carry-forward.

## Phase 12 - Budgeting + Hardening
| Increment | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **12A** Budgeting, Planning & Commitment Control (§4.18) | ✅ | `bud` schema. `Budget`/`BudgetLine` (fiscal year, **budget type/scenario**, department, **permanent version history** - a new active version supersedes the prior, never deletes; account × period lines). `IBudgetingService`: create (auto-versioned), **activate** (supersede prior active), **budget-vs-actual** (joins budget lines to **GL actuals read from period balances** by account/period - budgets never post to the GL), and **commitment/availability check** (available = budget − actual). `AddBudgeting` migration. Test: post 500 actual to Salaries → budget 1000 → BvA variance 500 / 50% used → availability passes 400, fails 600 → **trial balance unaffected by the budget**. |
| **12B** Hardening | ✅ | Full solution builds clean in **Debug and Release** with warnings-as-errors + XML docs on the core layer; **137 tests green** (93 unit + 9 integration + 35 accounting) against live PostgreSQL 18; every module posts through the central engine and every accounting test asserts the trial balance balances. |

**Phase 12 status: COMPLETE.** Cross-cutting budgeting with permanent version history and budget-vs-actual/commitment control (never contaminating the ledger), plus a clean Debug+Release build and full green test run. Deferred budgeting depth: departmental workflow/negotiation/appropriation, virement/rephasing, commitment from open POs/requisitions, utilisation-threshold notifications, the full `B`-series report set and sealed-hash version register.

## Project status - all planned phases (0–12) complete
Phases 0–12 of brief §16 are implemented, each posting through the central engine with the trial balance always balancing: **0** scaffold, **1** security + licensing + shell, **2** accounting core + posting engine, **3** reporting engine, **4** AR/AP/Cash Book, **5** inventory + procurement + workflow, **6** POS, **7** vouchers + SOP, **8** municipality + housing, **9** schools, **10** construction/job costing + weighbridge, **11** fixed assets + CRM, **11B** HR & payroll, **12** budgeting + hardening. Remaining work is breadth/depth within modules (see each phase's "deferred depth") and the deferred UI surfaces (WPF report designer, full-screen touch POS, permission-grid editor) - the accounting and domain cores are in place and tested.

## Module RCP - Cashiering & Receipting
Spec: [docs/modules/RECEIPTING.md](modules/RECEIPTING.md). Module code `RCP`, schema `rcp`.
| Increment | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **RCP-1 + RCP-2** Foundations + session/counter | ✅ | `rcp` schema. `CashierStation` (own receipt series), `CashierSession` (opening float per currency, blind cash-up variance, Open→Closed→Verified), `Receipt`/`ReceiptLine`/`ReceiptTender` (immutable, gapless number per station). `IPayerAccountProvider` (AR impl - resolves a debtor as a payer; a new register plugs in without touching the screen). `IReceiptingService`: **open session**, **post receipt** and **close session** (blind cash-up). The §14 posting patterns, each tested: debtor payment → **Dr cash / Cr debtor control via AR, allocated oldest-first (never income)**; sundry → Cr income vote; deposit → Cr deposit liability; suspense → Cr suspense control; overpayment remainder → credit on account; cash-up shortage/overage → Dr/Cr shortages. Guards: no zero/negative receipt, no receipt without an account or a code, tender must equal line total; **SoD: a cashier cannot verify their own cash-up**. `AddReceipting` migration. Test: bill 800 → mixed receipt (500 to debtor + 50 sundry) + 200 deposit → debtors 300, sundry income 50, deposit liability 200; blind cash-up expected 850 vs counted 840 → shortage 10; self-verify blocked, supervisor verifies; suspense untouched; TB balances. |
| **RCP-4** Allocation engine (strategies + preview) | ✅ | `ReceiptAllocationStrategy` (**OldestFirst / NewestFirst / LargestFirst / UserDirected**) on the receipt request; `PreviewAllocationAsync` shows the proposed spread **before commit** (never writes); user-directed allocations validate against the account's outstanding items (unknown item / over-item / over-payment refused). Test: 3 charges (Jan 100/Feb 200/Mar 300) → oldest-first preview of 250 = Jan 100 + Feb 150; newest-first = Mar 250; user-directs 150 to Feb → Feb 200→50, others untouched. |
| **RCP-5** Multi-currency at the counter | ✅ | Receipt carries a `Currency` (+ optional rate); a foreign-currency receipt posts through the engine converting to base. Same-currency debtor payments allocate; a foreign-currency debtor payment posts as an on-account credit (in its own currency) for the FX-realising allocation workbench. Test: EUR 100 sundry at 1.10 → Cr sundry income 110 base, Dr cash 110, TB balances. |
| **RCP-6 (part)** Suspense recovery path | ✅ | Unidentified money parks in the **suspense/unallocated control** (a `Suspense` receipt line → Cr suspense, never a guessed account); `AllocateFromSuspenseAsync` moves it out to the right debtor - **Dr suspense control / Cr debtor control** via the AR ledger, then applies it to the account's charges by strategy - a permissioned act with a **mandatory reason** (blank reason refused). Test: 300 unmatched deposit → sits in suspense → identified & allocated to a 400 charge → debtors 100, suspense cleared to nil, TB balances. |
| **RCP-7 (part)** Receipt reversal | ✅ | Built on the AR un-allocation primitive. `ISalesLedgerService.PostReceiptReversalAsync` (Dr AR control / Cr cash → a debit adjustment open item). `ReverseReceiptAsync`: un-allocates the receipt's matches (restoring the settled charges), posts the reversal debit and nets it against the restored receipt credit (both settle to nil), mirrors every coded line (Dr income/deposit/suspense / Cr cash), and **marks the receipt cancelled - never deleted**. Guards: mandatory reason; **a cashier cannot reverse their own receipt**; a cancelled receipt cannot be reversed again. `AddReceiptReversalAnchors` migration (persists the debtor open item + cash account on the receipt). Test: bill 800 → receipt 500 debtor + 50 sundry (debtors 300) → reverse → **debtors back to 800**, sundry income and cash net to nil, receipt is Cancelled, TB balances; self-reverse and blank-reason refused. |
| **RCP-7 (refunds)** | ✅ | `RefundCreditAsync` - refunds a payer's credit balance (overpayment/credit on account): Dr debtor control / Cr **bank** (paid from the bank/safe, never the till), then clears the account's outstanding credit items; refuses to refund more than the available credit. `RefundDepositAsync` - releases a deposit: Dr deposit liability (full), Cr bank (net), Cr forfeit income (any withheld). Both require a reason + approver. Tests: 200 overpayment refunded (credit cleared, bank −200); 500 deposit refunded with 50 forfeit → liability released, 50 to income, 450 from bank; over-refund refused. |
| **RCP-10** Banking & treasury | ✅ | `IBankingService` + `BankDeposit` (`rcp`): `BankCashAsync` moves takings into **cash-in-transit** (Dr CIT / Cr cash on hand, raising a deposit slip), `ConfirmBankedAsync` clears CIT to the bank on statement confirmation (Dr bank / Cr CIT) and makes the deposit immutable. `AddBankDeposits` migration; Cash-in-Transit account added to the Scenario. Test: 1000 banked → sits in CIT → confirmed → CIT cleared to nil, re-confirm refused, TB balances. |
| **RCP-6** Bulk / file-based imports | ✅ | `ImportBatch`/`ImportLine` (`rcp`) + `IReceiptImportService`. Imports a payment file, **auto-matches each line to an account by the code in the payment reference** (exactly-one-match wins; zero/ambiguous → suspense), receipts matched lines through the AR ledger (Dr bank / Cr debtor control, allocated oldest-first - never income) and parks unmatched money in the **suspense control** (Dr bank / Cr suspense). **Duplicate files are rejected by hash** (unique index). `AddReceiptImports` migration. Test: 3-line statement → 2 matched (Alice 500 settles, Bob 200 partial → debtors 100) + 1 unmatched (150 in suspense); re-import of the same hash rejected; TB balances. |
| **RCP-8** Agents + stationery control | ✅ | `Agent` (`rcp`) + `IAgentService`: an agent **collection** settles the debtor and holds the cash in an agent cash-control account (Dr agent control / Cr debtor control, oldest-first); **remittance** brings the net to bank, expenses the commission and clears the control (Dr bank net + Dr commission / Cr agent control gross); the agent's **collected − remitted** is its aged outstanding; over-remit refused. `StationeryBook`/`ManualReceiptCapture` + `IStationeryService`: register a pre-numbered book by serial range, **capture manual receipts by original serial** (out-of-range and duplicate serials refused), and a **gap report** of unaccounted serials. `AddAgentsAndStationery` migration. Tests: agent collects 500 (debtor 0, agent holds 500) → remits (commission 50, net 450, control cleared), over-remit refused; book 1001–1005, capture 1001/1002/1004 → gaps [1003,1005], duplicate & out-of-range refused. |
| **RCP-9** `RC` report catalogue | ✅ | All **53 RC-series report definitions (RC1–RC53)** seeded as system definitions in `rpt.report_definition` via `ReceiptingReports.All()` - built from a compact column spec into banded layouts (report header with company/title/params, page-header column labels, detail band of bound fields, money-column footer totals) for the listings, plus header/body documents for the receipt/dashboard/cash-up forms. Wired into `ReportCatalogueSeeder`. **Fixed a latent idempotency bug**: the `jsonb` `DefinitionJson` column is re-normalised by Postgres on write, so the raw-string compare always re-wrote every run - the seeder now compares canonically (round-trip through the serializer). Tests: unit (53 distinct RC codes, all well-formed + round-trip through the serializer, listings expose field detail bands) + integration (seeder persists 53 Receipting definitions and a second run changes nothing). Follow-up: live data providers to feed each `RC` command, and the designer-editable document layouts (thermal/A5/A4/multi-part). |
| **RCP-3 (cash movements)** | ✅ | `SessionCashMovement` (`rcp`) + `IReceiptingService.PickupAsync` (Dr safe/Cr cash on hand) and `PettyCashPayoutAsync` (Dr expense/Cr cash on hand). Both require a reason, only on an open session, and **reduce the drawer** so the blind cash-up expects correspondingly less. Wired **RC28** (pickups/drops/petty-cash report). `AddSessionCashMovements` migration; safe (1010) + petty-cash (6300) accounts added to the Scenario. Test: float 100 + cash 300 − pickup 250 − petty 20 → expected drawer 130 (variance 0); safe Dr 250, petty-cash expense Dr 20, TB balances; RC28 lists the movements. |
| **RCP-9 (refund register)** | ✅ | `RefundRecord` (`rcp`) persisted by `RefundCreditAsync`/`RefundDepositAsync` (payer, type, net amount, reason, approver, journal), so the **RC41 Refunds Register** renders live. `AddRefundRecords` migration. Test: overpay 150 → refund credit → RC41 shows "Refund Customer / Paid / 150.00". |
| **RCP-9 (cancellation reason)** | ✅ | `Receipt.Cancel(reason, byUserId)` now records the cancellation/reversal reason and user; `ReverseReceiptAsync` passes them, so **RC15 (Cancelled & Reversed Receipts)** shows the real reason and supervisor. `AddReceiptCancellationReason` migration. Reversal test asserts the reason/user are captured. |
| **RCP-9 (reprint log)** | ✅ | `ReceiptReprint` (`rcp`) + `IReceiptingService.ReprintReceiptAsync` (numbered per receipt, logged), feeding **RC16 (Receipt Reprint Log)**. `AddReceiptReprints` migration. Test: two reprints of a receipt → copy 1 and copy 2 on RC16. |
| **RCP-9 (arrangements)** | ✅ | `PaymentArrangement` (`rcp`) + `IReceiptingService.CaptureArrangementAsync` (total/instalment/start, status Active→Honoured/Defaulted), feeding **RC42 (Payment Arrangements)**. `AddPaymentArrangements` migration. Test: capture 1200 @ 100 → RC42 shows "ARR-1 / Active / 100.00"; instalment-over-total refused. |
| **RCP-9 (suspense allocation log)** | ✅ | `SuspenseAllocation` (`rcp`) persisted by `AllocateFromSuspenseAsync` (account, amount, reason, journal), feeding **RC30 (Suspense Allocation Report)**. `AddSuspenseAllocations` migration. The suspense integration test now also renders RC30 and asserts the reason + amount appear. |
| **RCP-7 (dishonours)** | ✅ | `DishonouredPayment` (`rcp`) + `IReceiptingService.RecordDishonourAsync`: reverses the original receipt (restoring the debt), raises a penalty on the payer's AR account when charged, and logs the event - feeding **RC34 (Dishonoured Cheque / Reversed Payment Register)**. `AddDishonouredPayments` migration. Test: cheque receipt 400 clears the debt → dishonour with 25 penalty → debt restored to **425**, RC34 lists cheque ref / 25 penalty / 400 amount, TB balances. |
| **RCP-9 (cash control recon)** | ✅ | **RC13 (Cash Control Account Reconciliation)** wired: the caller supplies the till/safe/transit/bank account codes and the provider sums each account's GL period-balance movement (base debit/credit) - till → safe → cash-in-transit → bank. No fabrication (accounts are named by the caller). Test: 300 cash + 250 pickup to safe → RC13 shows Cash on hand (1000) Dr 300/Cr 250 and Cash in safe (1010) Dr 250. |
| **RCP-9 (back-dated receipts)** | ✅ | **RC18 (Back-Dated Receipt Report)** wired with no new data: a receipt is back-dated when its business `Date` precedes its captured `CreatedOnUtc` date. Test: receipt dated 2026-02-01 (captured "now") shows on RC18 with amount 75. |
| **RCP-9 (audit trail)** | ✅ | **RC21 (Receipting Audit Trail)** reads the hash-chained `ChangeLog` filtered to the receipting entity types (receipt, session, deposit, refund, reprint, arrangement, suspense allocation, dishonour, import, agent, stationery, cash movement) - every insert/update is captured by the audit interceptor. Test: opening a session and posting a receipt appear on RC21 (Receipt + CashierSession rows). |
| **RCP-9 (dormant accounts)** | ✅ | **RC44 (Accounts With No Payment in N Months)** wired from existing data: debtors with a net outstanding AR balance whose last committed receipt is older than 3 months (or never). Test: a billed-but-unpaid debtor appears ("never"); one who paid within the window is excluded. |
| **RCP-9 (hourly volume)** | ✅ | **RC10 (Hourly Transaction Volume)** wired using the receipt capture timestamp (`CreatedOnUtc`) for the hour - transactions per hour per station (avg handling time deferred, needs per-transaction timing). Test: RC10 renders the capture hour and station. |
| **RCP-9 (service dimension)** | ✅ | Added a `Service` dimension to `ReceiptLine` (water/rates/refuse…), captured via `ReceiptLineRequest`, feeding **RC6 (Collection by Service)** - the council's "what did residents pay for" report. `AddReceiptLineService` migration. Test: a receipt tagged Water 80 + Refuse 30 → RC6 groups them. |
| **RCP-7 (pending tenders)** | ✅ | `PendingTender` (`rcp`) + a `Pending`/`PendingAccountCode` on the receipt request: a claimed bank transfer/cheque posts to an **uncleared-effects control** instead of cash/bank, is logged, and clears to the bank via `ConfirmPendingTenderAsync` (Dr bank/Cr uncleared) - feeding **RC33 (Pending Tenders, aged)**. `AddPendingTenders` migration. Test: a 500 pending transfer sits in 1050 and shows on RC33 → confirm → clears to bank 1100, 1050 nil, drops off RC33, re-confirm refused. |
| **RCP-15 (SoD violations log)** | ✅ | `SegregationViolation` (`rcp`) logged whenever a self-verify or self-reverse is refused, feeding **RC22 (Segregation of Duties Violations)**. `AddSegregationViolations` migration. Test: a cashier's blocked self-verify is refused and appears on RC22. Remaining empty RC reports need ward tagging (RC9/RC49), related-party ownership (RC23), field-collector (RC47/48) or prepaid-token (RC52) entities, or are document layouts (RC1/2/24/35/36/37/53). |
| **RCP-3 (remaining)** | ⬜ | WPF receipting screen + multi-register live search; cross-currency debtor allocation realising FX at the counter; field-collector offline client; still-empty RC reports needing new capture (hourly time, pending tenders, service/ward tagging, SoD/related-party logs, prepaid tokens). |

**Module RCP status:** the transaction/accounting core, the allocation engine (strategies + preview), multi-currency posting and the suspense recovery path are in place and tested - the "a receipt against a debtor never credits income" rule holds, blind cash-up posts variance, allocation strategies are honoured with a pre-commit preview, unidentified money is recoverable from suspense, and SoD/validation guards fail closed. UI, file imports, cross-currency debtor allocation, reversals/refunds/banking, agents and the `RC` reports remain.

## Reporting - live data providers (whole-system)
| Increment | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Vertical report providers | ✅ | `VerticalReportDataProvider` extends live rendering beyond the ledgers/receipting to the vertical modules, with new seeded system definitions: **INV-VAL** Stock Valuation (from `IInventoryService`), **FA-REG** Fixed Asset Register, **PAY-SUM** Payroll Summary, **MUN-PROP** Property Register, **BUD-BVA** Budget vs Actual (from `IBudgetingService`). Test: receive stock, register an asset, seed an employee payslip, a municipal property and an active budget with an actual, then render all five to HTML and assert the live figures (WIDGET/200, VH-9/24,000, Ann Employee/1,800 net, STAND-X/100,000, 6100/1,000 budget). |
| POS + job-costing providers | ✅ | `PosJobReportDataProvider` + seeded definitions: **POS-SUM** POS Sales Summary (Sale net/tax/gross), **JOB-PROF** Job Profitability (contract value/budget/actual/certified/%complete), **JOB-IPC** Progress Certificates (gross/retention/net payable). Test: a completed POS sale and a certified contract → render all three to HTML and assert the live figures (POS-0001/57.50; C-500/200,000 actual/25%; IPC-9/250,000 gross/225,000 net). |
| Housing / school / procurement registers | ✅ | `RegistersReportDataProvider` + seeded definitions: **HSE-STAND** Housing Stand Register (status, cost, price, holder), **SCH-STU** Student Register (grade, boarder, bursary), **POP-OPEN** Open Purchase Orders (supplier, status, value; excludes closed). Test: a stand, an enrolled bursary student and an open PO → render all three to HTML and assert the live figures (ERF-100/25,000/Available; ADM-100/Pupil One/300; PO-100/Bolt Supplier/100). **Live reporting now spans every module with read-side data.** |
| Generic report runner + data providers | ✅ | `IReportRunner` - one entry point for **every** report in the catalogue: resolves the definition by code (company → system, with a built-in fallback), dispatches the definition's data-source command to the `IReportDataProvider` that handles it, and renders to HTML/PDF/CSV/text through the banded engine. Providers wired across the system: **`GeneralLedgerReportDataProvider`** (TrialBalance, IncomeStatement, BalanceSheet, NominalActivity), **`TradingReportDataProvider`** (AgedDebtors, AgedCreditors, CustomerStatement), **`ReceiptingReportDataProvider`** - now backs **every RC report with real data available**: RC3 (daily collection summary), RC4 (day book), RC5 (by revenue vote), RC7 (by tender/channel), RC8 (by currency), RC11 (cashier productivity), RC12 (receipting↔GL reconciliation), RC14 (till variance), RC15 (cancelled receipts), RC17 (receipt-number gap analysis), RC20 (stationery control), RC25 (daily banking summary), RC26 (cash-in-transit ageing), RC29 (suspense register), RC31 (import batches), RC32 (import exceptions), RC38 (payment history), RC39 (credit balances/overpayments), RC40 (deposits held), RC43 (top payers), RC45 (agent reconciliation), RC46 (agent ageing), RC51 (collection trend). The remaining RC codes are either document layouts (RC1/RC2/RC24/RC35–37/RC53) or need data not yet captured (hourly time, service/ward tagging, reprint/pickup/arrangement/refund logs, field-collector entities) and render their layout empty. Added system definitions **GL-IS / GL-BS / AR-AGE / AP-AGE** (compact `SystemReports.Listing` builder) and seeded them. An unknown code returns `Report.NotFound`. Test: seed catalogue + post an invoice + register an agent, then render GL-TB, GL-IS, AR-AGE and RC45 as HTML (all show the live figures - 1,234 debtor/sales, the customer, the agent) and AR-AGE as CSV; missing code fails cleanly. |

## Optional module - Restaurant / Hospitality (`res`)
| Increment | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Restaurant module | ✅ | Optional hospitality module (`res` schema, module code `RES`). **`MenuItem`** is a sellable composite (price + revenue/COGS accounts + tax) with a **recipe** of `RecipeLine` ingredients that point at stock-tracked inventory `Item`s - the menu item is never stocked; its ingredients are. **`RestaurantTable`** (Free/Occupied) + **`TableOrder`/`TableOrderLine`** (Open→Billed) give the table function. `IRestaurantService`: create menu item (+recipe), create table, **open order** (table must be free → Occupied), **add item** at menu price, **bill order** - recognises revenue (Dr cash / Cr revenue by account / Cr VAT) and **cost of sales by exploding each line's recipe into moving-average stock issues** via `IInventoryService`, then frees the table. `AddRestaurant` migration; `RES` period opened in the Scenario. Reports **RES-MENU** (menu list) + **RES-TBL** (table status) wired. **Design decision:** menu items sell through the restaurant order→bill flow (the hospitality equivalent of a POS sale); COGS is the recipe explosion, so menu items stay off the stock ledger while ingredients remain valued at moving average - the retail `pos` module still sells inventory items directly. Test: Cheeseburger @50 (recipe bun@2 + patty@8) → order 2 → bill: revenue 100, VAT 15, **COGS 20**, stock 100→98 each, table freed, TB balances; second order on the occupied table refused. |
| Table management | ✅ | `CancelOrderAsync` (void an open order - no GL, frees the table, reason required) and `TransferOrderAsync` (move an open order to a free table, freeing the original). Test: open on T1 → transfer to T2 (T1 free, T2 occupied, order.TableId=T2) → cancel (order Cancelled, T2 freed); blank-reason cancel refused. |
| Split bills + service charge | ✅ | Per-line `BilledQuantity`/`RemainingQuantity` enable **split billing**: `BillPartialAsync` bills specified `(line, quantity)` portions (revenue + prorated tax + recipe COGS for that portion); the order closes and the table frees only when fully billed. Both `BillOrderAsync` and `BillPartialAsync` take an optional **service charge** (Cr service-charge income, added to the gross). `AddOrderLineBilledQuantity` migration; Service Charge Income account added to the Scenario. Test: 4 pizzas - bill 1 (net 40, not fully billed), over-split refused, then bill the rest with a 12 service charge (net 120, service 12, gross 132, fully billed) → total revenue 160, service income 12, COGS 12, stock 100→96, table freed, TB balances. |
| Tips + kitchen order tickets | ✅ | **Tips** are staff money, not house income: billing accepts an optional `tipAmount`/`tipsPayableAccountCode` → Dr cash / **Cr Tips Payable (liability)**; `PayTipsAsync` clears it (Dr tips payable / Cr bank). **Kitchen Order Tickets**: `KitchenTicket`/`KitchenTicketLine` + `TableOrderLine.Fired`; `FireOrderAsync` fires the not-yet-fired lines as a KOT (course firing - new items fire again), `AdvanceTicketAsync` moves Sent→Preparing→Ready→Served (never backwards). `AddKitchenTickets` migration; Tips Payable account added to the Scenario. Test: fire order → advance to Ready (backward refused, re-fire refused) → bill steak 90 + 10 tip (revenue 90, **Cr tips payable 10**, gross 100) → pay tips out → liability cleared; TB balances. |
| Menu availability + KOT report | ✅ | `SetMenuAvailabilityAsync` **"86"s** a menu item (unavailable items are refused on `AddItemAsync`); RES-MENU now shows an **Available** column. New **RES-KOT** report lists open kitchen tickets (not yet served) with status, item count and sent date. Test: 86 the soup → order refused → bring it back → order + fire → RES-MENU shows availability, RES-KOT lists the open ticket. |
| Tip pooling + menu-engineering report | ✅ | **Tip pooling**: `TipAllocation` (Owed/Paid) records who within the Tips Payable liability is owed what. `DistributeTipsAsync` splits a pool by staff **weight** (last share absorbs the rounding remainder, so shares sum exactly; no GL - the liability already exists); `PayTipAllocationAsync` pays one staff member (Dr tips payable / Cr bank) and marks it paid (re-pay refused). New **RES-TIPS** report lists allocations (staff, date, status, amount). `AddTipAllocations` migration. **Menu engineering (RES-MIX)**: per menu item over a period - QtySold (billed lines), Revenue, recipe **standard cost** (ingredients' weighted-average cost), Margin, and a **Class** (Star / Plowhorse / Puzzle / Dog by popularity × profitability vs the menu averages). Tests: distribute 30 tip pool 2:1 → 20/10 owed, pay the 20 (liability 30→10), RES-TIPS shows both; sell 5 burgers + 1 salad → RES-MIX marks the burger a **Star** (margin 200) and the salad a **Dog**; TB balances throughout. |
| Table reservations | ✅ | `TableReservation` (Booked → Seated / Cancelled / NoShow; guest, phone, party size, `ReservedFor`, notes) - operational only, no GL. `ReserveTableAsync` refuses a party larger than the table seats and a clash with another live booking on the same table within a 90-minute turn-time window; `SeatReservationAsync` opens a table order on the reserved (free) table and links it; `CancelReservationAsync` (reason required) and `MarkReservationNoShowAsync` close a booking (each requires it to still be Booked). New **RES-RESV** report lists upcoming (Booked) bookings by time. `AddTableReservations` migration. Tests: oversize party + 30-min clash refused, 2-hour gap allowed; seat → order opens, table Occupied, booking drops off the upcoming list, re-seat refused; cancel needs a reason, cancel/no-show require a booked reservation, freeing a slot lets it be re-booked. |
| Waitlist + table combining | ✅ | **Waitlist**: `WaitlistEntry` (Waiting → Seated / Left / NoShow; guest, phone, party, quoted wait, `AddedOn`, notes) queues walk-ins - no GL. `AddToWaitlistAsync`, `SeatFromWaitlistAsync` (opens an order on a chosen free table + links it), `RemoveFromWaitlistAsync` (left) and `MarkWaitlistNoShowAsync` (each needs a Waiting entry). New **RES-WAIT** report lists the queue in join order. **Table combining**: `TableGroup` (Active/Released, label, primary, combined seats, member count) + `RestaurantTable.GroupId` join ≥2 free, ungrouped tables. `CombineTablesAsync` (lowest table number = primary) refuses <2 tables / occupied / already-grouped; the whole group **occupies and frees together** (order opens on the primary via `OpenOrderAsync`, which now guards non-primary members and occupies siblings; billing/cancel/transfer free the group). `UncombineTablesAsync` releases a **free** group and unlinks the tables (a seated group is refused). New **RES-COMB** report lists active groups. `AddWaitlistAndTableGroups` migration. Tests: queue three, one leaves, seat one (table Occupied, seated/left drop off the list, seating an occupied table refused); combine C1+C2 → 8 seats, order on non-primary refused, opening on the primary occupies both, uncombine-while-seated refused, bill frees both, then release unlinks and the tables can be re-combined; TB balances. |
| Floor-plan sections | ✅ | `FloorSection` (code, name, description, assigned server, active flag) zones the floor plan; `RestaurantTable.SectionId` puts each table in a section - operational only, no GL. `CreateSectionAsync` (unique code per company), `AssignTableToSectionAsync` (section or null to unassign; validates the section exists), `AssignSectionServerAsync` (server or null) and `SetSectionActiveAsync` (open/close a section for service). New **RES-SECT** floor-plan report (per section: tables, seats, live occupancy, assigned server, active) and **RES-SSALES** section-sales report (billed net/tax/gross + order count by section over a period, tables with no section bucketed as "(Unassigned)"). `AddFloorSections` migration. Test: two sections (duplicate code refused), assign tables + a real server user, seat a patio table → RES-SECT shows 6 patio seats and the server's name; bill 80 → RES-SSALES shows Patio net 80; close the bar + unassign a table; TB balances. |
| Coursing | ✅ | `MenuCourse` (Drinks → Starter → Main → Dessert) on `TableOrderLine`; `AddItemAsync` takes a `course` (default Main). `FireCourseAsync` fires only the not-yet-fired lines of a single course as its own KOT (`KitchenTicket.Course`), leaving later courses to fire when the table is ready; `FireOrderAsync` still fires everything still unfired. Operational only, no GL. `AddOrderCoursing` migration. Test: order soup/steak/cake across three courses → fire starter (re-fire refused), fire main, then "fire the rest" picks up only the dessert; each KOT carries its course and single item; nothing left to fire at the end. |
| Voids &amp; comps | ✅ | `TableOrderLine.IsVoided`/`IsComped` (+ reasons). `VoidLineAsync` removes a line before billing (excluded from the bill and totals, consumes no stock); `CompLineAsync` gives it free - **still made, so cost of sales is recognised via the recipe, but no revenue**. Both need a reason, an open order and nothing already billed on the line. Billing skips voided lines, zeroes comped revenue, and **skips the revenue post entirely when the guest owes nothing** (a fully-comped bill still explodes COGS and closes). New **RES-VOID** register lists voided/comped lines with reasons over a period. `AddVoidsAndComps` migration. Tests: void fries + comp dessert on a 50 burger → revenue 50, COGS 8 (burger recipe), RES-VOID shows both reasons; fully-comped steak → gross 0, COGS 12, order Billed, revenue nil; TB balances. |
| Bill discounts | ✅ | `BillOrderAsync`/`BillPartialAsync` take an optional `discountAmount`/`discountAccountCode`. The discount is a **contra-revenue** ("Discounts Allowed"): revenue is still credited at the full menu price, the discount is **Dr Discounts Allowed**, and cash is debited net of it - so the reduction is visible rather than hidden in a lower sale. Refuses a discount larger than the bill; a full discount posts no cash line but still balances. No migration (billing-time param); Discounts Allowed account added to the Scenario. Test: 20 off a 100 meal → guest pays 80, revenue 100, Discounts Allowed Dr 20, cash Dr 80, over-large discount refused, TB balances. |
| Split-by-seat billing | ✅ | `TableOrderLine.SeatNumber`; `AddItemAsync` takes an optional `seatNumber` and `AssignLineToSeatAsync` (re)assigns a line to a seat. `BillSeatAsync` bills one seat's not-yet-billed, non-voided lines as its own split (revenue + prorated tax + recipe COGS; optional service charge / tip / discount per seat); the order closes and the table frees when the last seat is settled - reuses the split-bill `PostBillAsync`. `AddOrderLineSeat` migration. Test: seat 1 (steak + wine 130) and seat 2 (salad 30) on one table → bill seat 1 (not fully billed, table still Occupied, re-bill refused), bill seat 2 (fully billed, table freed), revenue 160 across the two seat bills; TB balances. |
| Auto-gratuity for large parties | ✅ | `TableOrder.PartySize` (set at `OpenOrderAsync`, carried through reservation/waitlist seating). `RestaurantPolicy` (one row per company) holds the auto-gratuity threshold, percent and service-charge account; `SetAutoGratuityPolicyAsync` upserts it. `QuoteAutoGratuityAsync` returns an `AutoGratuityQuote` (Applies / Amount / account) = policy% of the still-billable net when the party meets the threshold - the POS passes the amount as the service charge at billing, so it books through the existing service-charge-income plumbing (no new GL path). `AddRestaurantPolicyAndPartySize` migration. Test: policy 6+ → 10%; party of 6 on a 200 tab quotes 20 to 4600 (party of 2 quotes nothing), bill with the quote → service charge 20, gross 220, GL shows food revenue 200 + service-charge income 20; TB balances. |
| Menu modifiers | ✅ | `OrderLineModifier` (description, price delta, optional extra ingredient + qty) customizes an order line - "extra cheese" +3, "no onions" 0. `AddItemModifierAsync` (order open, line not billed) attaches one; the line's `EffectiveUnitPrice`/`LineNet` include the deltas. Billing recognises revenue on the modified price and, for ingredient modifiers, **issues the extra stock into COGS** alongside the recipe. `AddOrderLineModifiers` migration. Test: 2 burgers @50 + "no onions" (0) + "extra cheese" (+3, 1 slice each) → line net 106, bill COGS 3 (2 cheese @1.50), cheese stock 100→98, revenue 106; TB balances. |
| Order types (takeaway / delivery) | ✅ | `OrderType` (DineIn / Takeaway / Delivery) on `TableOrder`; `TableId` is now **nullable** so off-premise orders occupy no floor. `OpenOffPremiseOrderAsync` opens a takeaway/delivery order (customer name/phone, no table; refuses DineIn). Items are added and billed through the same path - billing frees a table only when the order has one. New **RES-OTYPE** report splits billed net/tax/gross by fulfilment type over a period. `AddOrderTypes` migration. Test: dine-in (60) + takeaway (120, no table, Ada) + delivery (60, Grace) → all bill, off-premise TableId null, RES-OTYPE shows the three types with takeaway net 120, total revenue 240; TB balances. |
| Delivery dispatch | ✅ | Delivery orders carry a `DeliveryStatus` (Pending → Assigned → OutForDelivery → Delivered; Failed from any active state), a `DriverUserId` and a `DeliveryAddress` (set at open). `AssignDeliveryDriverAsync` (Pending → Assigned) and `AdvanceDeliveryAsync` (forward-only; refuses a backward move or any change once Delivered/Failed; non-delivery orders have no dispatch state). New **RES-DELIV** report lists in-progress deliveries with customer, address, driver and status. `AddDeliveryDispatch` migration. Operational only, no GL. Test: assign a real driver user → Assigned (backward refused, takeaway rejected), RES-DELIV shows the delivery with driver + address, advance Out-for-delivery → Delivered (further changes refused), delivered order drops off the list. |
| Waiter reassignment + server sales | ✅ | `ReassignWaiterAsync` hands an open order to another waiter (shift change / section handover; billed orders refused). New **RES-SERVER** report totals billed net/tax/gross and order count by the order's current waiter over a period (tip-out / performance), names resolved from the user directory. No migration (existing `WaiterUserId`, read-side report). Test: two orders opened by Ana, one handed to Ben → bill both, RES-SERVER credits Ana 100 and Ben 50, reassigning a billed order refused; TB balances. |
| House-account billing (charge to room) | ✅ | `BillToHouseAccountAsync` charges an open order to a customer's account instead of taking a tender: raises a **sales invoice on the debtor through `ISalesLedgerService`** (Dr AR control / Cr revenue / Cr tax, ageing on the customer like any receivable), recognises recipe cost of sales, and closes the order / frees the table. Comped lines cost but do not invoice; a fully-comped order raises no invoice. Refactored the recipe+modifier COGS explosion and the "close & free table" logic into shared helpers reused by both cash and house-account billing. No migration. Test: charge a 100 + VAT 15 steak plate to "Room 204" → AR control Dr 115, revenue 100, VAT 15, COGS 12, **no cash movement**, and it ages 115 on the debtor; TB balances. |
| Gift-card tender | ✅ | `BillToVoucherAsync` settles a bill with a gift card: the tender debits the **gift-card liability** (Dr liability / Cr revenue / Cr tax, exactly as a cash tender against that account) and the card balance is reduced by the same amount via `IVoucherService.RedeemAsync`. Checks the card covers the whole bill first (partial-tender left for later); insufficient balance leaves the order open. Recipe COGS recognised, order closed and table freed. No migration. Test: issue a $100 card, pay a 60 bill → gift-card liability nets to 40 credit, revenue 60, card balance 40; a second 60 bill exceeds the remaining 40 and is refused (order stays open); TB balances. |
| Menu price levels | ✅ | `MenuItemPrice` (per-item alternate prices by named level - LUNCH, HAPPYHOUR, DELIVERY…). `SetMenuItemPriceLevelAsync` upserts a level price; `AddItemAsync` takes an optional `priceLevel` and `MenuItem.PriceFor(level)` returns the level price or falls back to the base price (unknown/blank level → base). The chosen price is captured on the order line, so billing is unchanged. `AddMenuItemPriceLevels` migration. Test: beer base 8, HAPPYHOUR set to 5 then updated to 4 → order one at happy-hour (4), one base (8), one unknown level (→8); bill net 20; TB balances. |
| Category sales report | ✅ | New **RES-CAT** report totals billed qty/net/tax/gross by menu category (joining lines → menu items) over a period; items with no category bucket as "(Uncategorised)". Read-side only, no migration. Test: steak (Mains) ×2 = 180 and wine (Drinks) = 40 → RES-CAT shows Mains 180 and Drinks 40; TB balances. |
| Menu cost card report | ✅ | New **RES-COST** report shows every menu item's price, recipe cost (from the ingredients' weighted-average stock cost), gross margin and food-cost %. Read-side only, no migration - a pricing/menu-design tool. Test: cheeseburger priced 40 with recipe bun @2 + patty @8 → cost 10, margin 30, food cost 25%. |
| Persisted bills (audit trail) | ✅ | `RestaurantBill` captures the money breakdown (net, tax, service charge, tip, discount, gross, COGS, revenue journal) each time an order or a split is billed - written by every billing path (`PostBillAsync` and the house-account path). New **RES-BILLS** report lists all bills over a period. `AddRestaurantBills` migration. Foundation for future bill reversal. Test: bill a 100 + VAT 15 order with a 12 service charge and 8 discount → gross 119; a `RestaurantBill` row persists net 100 / tax 15 / service 12 / discount 8 / gross 119 with a revenue journal, and RES-BILLS shows the order at 119. |
| Bill reversal | ✅ | `ReverseBillAsync` reverses a tender-settled bill rung in error: the bill now also captures its **revenue journal lines** (`RestaurantBillPosting`) and **cost-of-sales stock issues** (`RestaurantBillIssue`); reversal posts the exact inverse revenue journal (refunding the tender, unwinding revenue/tax/service/tip/discount) and calls the new `IInventoryService.ReverseIssueAsync` for each issue to return the stock at its original cost. Refuses a bill already reversed, a house-account bill (use the AR credit note) or a blank reason. `AddBillReversal` migration. Test: bill a 90 + VAT 15 steak plate (cash 105, COGS 12, stock 50→49) → reverse → revenue/VAT/cash/COGS all net to zero, stock back to 50, re-reversal refused; TB balances. |
| Void/comp manager authorisation | ✅ | `TableOrderLine.AuthorizedByUserId`; `VoidLineAsync`/`CompLineAsync` take an optional `authorizedByUserId` recorded on the line for accountability. The **RES-VOID** report gains an **Authorised** column resolving the manager's name from the user directory. `AddVoidCompAuthoriser` migration. Test: void + comp two lines authorised by "Morgan Manager" → the line stores the id and RES-VOID shows both reasons and the manager's name (existing void/comp tests still pass - the param is optional). |
| Bar-tab spend limit | ✅ | `TableOrder.SpendLimit` + `SetOrderSpendLimitAsync` pre-authorise a tab; `AddItemAsync` refuses an item whose net would push the running tab over the limit (the running total includes modifiers, excludes voided/comped). Raise or clear the limit to let it flow again. `AddOrderSpendLimit` migration. Test: $50 tab → 4 beers (40) ok, a round of 2 (→60) refused, 1 more (→50 exactly) ok, another refused; raise to 100 → 3 more ok; clear → unbounded; final net 580. |
| Auto-86 (low-stock availability) | ✅ | `RefreshMenuAvailabilityAsync(warehouse)` reconciles menu availability against live stock: a recipe-backed item whose ingredients cannot cover one serving is auto-**86'd** (`IsAvailable=false`, so `AddItemAsync` refuses it), and one that can is brought back; recipe-less items (drinks) are left untouched. Returns the number changed. Read-then-update, no GL, no migration. Test: burger (1 patty) with 2 patties in stock → available (refresh reports 0 changes); order + bill 2 burgers to drain stock → refresh 86's the burger (juice untouched), ordering it now refused; restock → refresh brings it back. |
| Split-tender billing | ✅ | `BillWithTendersAsync` settles a bill across several tenders (part cash + part card, two cards, etc.): the `TenderLine[]` must sum to the gross (net + tax + service charge + tip − discount); the revenue journal debits each tender account instead of a single cash line. Threaded an optional tender list through the shared `PostBillAsync` (existing single-tender callers unchanged). Mismatched tenders leave the order open. No migration. Test: a 100 + VAT 15 bill → tenders summing to 100 refused; 70 cash + 45 bank = 115 posts (cash Dr 70, bank Dr 45, revenue 100, VAT 15); TB balances. |
| Stock wastage | ✅ | `WastageRecord` + `RecordWastageAsync` writes off spoiled/wasted stock: **issues the ingredient at moving average** (Dr COS / Cr stock) then **reclassifies the cost to a wastage expense account** (Dr Wastage / Cr COS) - net effect Dr Wastage / Cr stock, cost of sales untouched. Requires a reason, a positive quantity and an item with a COS account. New **RES-WASTE** report lists wastage (date, item, qty, value, reason) over a period. `AddStockWastage` migration; Stock Wastage account (5200) added to the Scenario. Test: receive 100 lettuce @2, waste 10 (reason mandatory, positive qty) → value 20, stock 100→90, **Wastage Dr 20, COS nets to zero**, RES-WASTE shows the write-off; TB balances. |

## Decisions / deviations from brief
- **.NET 10** not .NET 8 (ADR-0001) - only SDK on the machine.
- **PostgreSQL 18** dev, min supported 16 (ADR-0002).
- **Product name One4All** not Meridian (ADR-0003).
- **No Docker** on the dev machine → integration tests fall back to the local PG18 instance; Testcontainers path kept for CI.

## Open questions for the product owner
- _(resolved)_ Local `postgres` password is `root`; stored in `src/One4All.Server/appsettings.Development.json` (gitignored). DB `one4all` created and migrated.
